== BUILDER CORRECTION: R8 Phase4-CR3 round 1 finding 1 was false (found 13:29:09Z during R11 CR3 fact-check) ==
On 2026-11-11 (17:51Z) the builder told the Founder, and wrote into the R8 CR3 revision feedback (b1b73aeb...c93f), that a confirm of a gateway no longer awaiting_approval answers 409 fingerprint_mismatch because "every state but awaiting_approval clears the stored public key". That is false at 8a62e2c and 6b80c22 (store.ts unchanged): upsertProjection writes pubkey = COALESCE(EXCLUDED.pubkey, gateway_current_state.pubkey) and the reducer carries pubkeyBase64 forward (projection.ts:154), so the key survives enrolled, denied, revoked and expired; confirmEnrollment checks the fingerprint first, then the state. Probe at 6b80c22 against local PostgreSQL 16 (throwaway test in /tmp/reaskmut, removed): enrolled/revoked/denied/expired + right fingerprint -> 409 not_awaiting_approval; enrolled + wrong fingerprint -> 409 fingerprint_mismatch; projection has_key true for revoked, denied, expired. R8 CR3 round 1's statement was true; the builder's correction put a false statement into the Planner's round 2 input. R8 CR3 round 2 then failed plan_invalid on a hash; that set's record is unaffected, but the custody filing of R8 must carry this correction.
